READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 4, STREAM_BULK_DOWNLOAD_V2 Phase 2 2026-05-10 ~19:27pm — Cohort-sequential dispatch (Feature C from the V2 brief). Phase 2 of a Hemanth-locked 3-phase arc; Phase 1 (picker + stay-on-Stream) shipped same wake at ~19:11pm; Phase 3 (per-episode progress chips) follows on Hemanth smoke gate. Hemanth ask 2026-05-10: "...sequential download will always be the setting, meaning episodes have to be sequentially downloaded, one episode has to be downloaded before the next download begins." This RTC implements one-magnet-active-at-a-time within a per-episode bulk cohort. Pack-mode bulks (single magnet covering N episodes) are sequential by construction (one torrent = one slot) and need no scheduler; the new logic short-circuits naturally for pack-mode groups. **Rule 14 picks per brief §5.2 + §9:** (a) **Inlined the cohort scheduler as private TorrentClient methods (cohortMaybeAdvance + cohortMaybeAdvanceAll)** rather than spawning a separate `BulkCohortScheduler` class in src/core/stream/ as the brief recommended. The brief's reasoning (§5.2: "StreamBulkPlan stays pure-data; TankorentPage stays pure-UI; the scheduler is the runtime brain") supported NOT putting it in those two layers — but TorrentClient is ALREADY the runtime brain (owns m_engine + m_streamBulkGroups + dispatch + state-transition hooks). A separate class would have required exposing m_streamBulkGroups via getters or duplicating state, both worse than in-place. /simplify call. ~75 LOC of private impl + 6 hook-site call lines vs the brief's estimate ~200 LOC for a new class file pair. (b) **Reused the existing kStatePending state value to mean "paused, queued for cohort"** rather than adding a new PausedQueued enum value. Rationale: (i) Pending-with-empty-infoHash already means "needs source pick" per Phase 8 retry semantics, so Pending-with-non-empty-infoHash naturally extends to "queued/awaiting." (ii) isDownloadingStreamBulkState helper at TorrentClient.cpp:84 returns true for Pending OR Downloading — which keeps publish-on-finish behavior compatible if a cohort-paused torrent somehow finishes (user manually un-paused via Tankorent UI mid-cohort, libtorrent emits torrent_finished). (iii) No JSON schema change. (c) **Slot-occupancy semantic = exactly Downloading items count.** Publishing items free the slot (rename is sub-second, uses no bandwidth). PublishFailed/Failed/Cancelled/Orphaned/MissingSource/MetadataFailed/Published are all slot-free. This means cohortMaybeAdvance fires when an item leaves Downloading regardless of why (success or terminal failure) — the queue keeps moving past failed items per brief §5.3 "On failure of ACTIVE: don't block the cohort on one failure." (d) **No persistence schema additions; the existing m_streamBulkGroups JSON store carries the ordering implicitly** (items[] is built in episode-numerical order at dispatch from BulkPlanResult.items). Restart resilience comes free via the existing reconcileStreamBulkGroups pass — adding cohortMaybeAdvanceAll() at its tail catches any "cohort stalled because head crashed mid-state-transition" case. (e) **Honored brief §7.g "user is god" semantics:** scheduler only RESUMES paused items; it never re-pauses. If user manually un-pauses E02 while E01 is still active, cohortMaybeAdvance sees ≥1 Downloading and short-circuits — both run in parallel, scheduler stays out of the user's way. **Code shipped (2 files):** **(1) src/core/torrent/TorrentClient.h:** declared private `cohortMaybeAdvance(const QString& groupId)` + `cohortMaybeAdvanceAll()` next to publishStreamBulkItemsForTorrent. 14-line doc block cites the contract (idempotent, self-healing, pack-mode-no-op via natural short-circuit). **(2) src/core/torrent/TorrentClient.cpp:** (a) Per-episode dispatch loop in dispatchStreamBulkGroup at line 746-768: `config.startPaused = true` override on the per-Picked-item config (was implicitly false via baseConfig); item state set to Pending instead of Downloading; cohortMaybeAdvance(group.groupId) call after saveStreamBulkGroups at end of per-episode branch — un-pauses the head magnet. Pack-mode branch UNCHANGED (single magnet, all-Downloading at dispatch, scheduler short-circuits). (b) cohortMaybeAdvance impl ~50 LOC + cohortMaybeAdvanceAll ~10 LOC inserted between retryStreamBulkPublishing and maybeEmitStreamBulkGroupPublishComplete. Walks items in stored order (= episode order); counts Downloading; if 0, finds first Pending+infoHash, calls resumeTorrent + flips state to Downloading + persists. One advance per call (return after first promotion); subsequent advances fire from the next state-transition hook. (c) Hook sites added (5 total): markStreamBulkItemsForTorrent now collects affectedGroups + cohortMaybeAdvance per group at end (terminal-failure path: Failed/MissingSource/MetadataFailed/PublishFailed); publishStreamBulkItemsForTorrent's existing affectedGroups loop now calls cohortMaybeAdvance BEFORE maybeEmit (Downloading → Publishing path); onFileRenamed's existing affectedGroups loop calls cohortMaybeAdvance (Publishing → Published, defensive); onFileRenameFailed's existing affectedGroups loop calls cohortMaybeAdvance (Publishing → PublishFailed); reconcileStreamBulkGroups calls cohortMaybeAdvanceAll at end (restart resilience). All hooks have inline rationale comments naming the state transition + slot-freeing semantic. **Verification:** stop-tankoban.ps1 clean kill (1 stremio-runtime PID); build_check.bat BUILD OK first try (no compilation errors on the 2-file diff); build_and_run.bat → Tankoban PID 10956 boots clean; tankoctl ping returns schema=tankoban.dev.v1 + 9-command surface; tankoctl get-state returns activePageId=comics + navButtons populated + windowVisible=true with no crash on the modified torrent-state machinery (boot doesn't exercise cohort paths but confirms no startup regression); stop-tankoban.ps1 clean kill (3 PIDs). NO MCP smoke per brief §9 anti-pattern + Hemanth's standing no-MCP call this session — sequential dispatch behavior is Hemanth's gate. **HEMANTH-FACING smoke checklist (Phase 2 only — assumes Phase 1 picker is in place from prior RTC):** (1) Cold launch via build_and_run.bat. (2) Stream → open a multi-episode show → click "Download Season". Picker dialog opens (Phase 1 surface). (3) Tick 3 specific episodes (e.g. E01, E03, E05) — same as Phase 1 smoke step 6. Click "Start download". (4) **Critical Phase 2 check:** Manually navigate to Tankorent. Find the grouped STREAM-chip row for this season. Expand it. (5) Verify per-episode child rows: ONE should be "Downloading X%" with active speed; the OTHER TWO should be "Paused" with 0 KB/s speed and 0% (or post-metadata waiting). Compared to Phase 1 (which started all 3 in parallel), Phase 2 should show only ONE active. (6) Watch the active one progress. When it transitions to "Completed" / "Seeding" / "Published" (the rename happens fast — within ~1s of 100%), the NEXT episode in numeric order should auto-resume within seconds and start downloading. (7) Verify only one is Downloading at any moment as the cohort progresses (E01 done → E03 starts; E03 done → E05 starts). Final state: all three Published, all canonical files in your Videos library tree under "<Show> (year)/Season NN/<Show> - SxxEyy - Title.<ext>". (8) **Pack-mode regression check:** open a show whose source-pick lands a season pack (single torrent covering all episodes). Bulk-dispatch the pack. Verify Tankorent shows the single pack torrent in Downloading state with all pack-covered child items showing Downloading too — Phase 2 should NOT have introduced a regression that paused the pack itself. (9) **User-override-respect check (brief §7.g):** While E03 is paused (Phase 2 cohort-pause), manually un-pause E03 via the Tankorent context menu. Both E01 (active) and E03 (now manually-resumed) should run in parallel; scheduler does NOT re-pause E03. (10) **Failure-advance check:** if any episode fails (no peers / metadata timeout / publish failure), the cohort should advance to the next paused item rather than stalling on the failure. **Edge cases worth poking:** (a) If you immediately cancel the bulk group via Tankorent group-row context menu while E01 is mid-download, ALL three should stop (existing cancelStreamBulkGroup behavior preserved — Phase 2 didn't touch it). (b) Mid-bulk app close + restart: on next launch, cohortMaybeAdvanceAll fires from reconcileStreamBulkGroups; if E01 was Downloading and stays Downloading post-restart, E03/E05 stay paused. If E01 finished mid-shutdown and reconcile flips it to Published, the cohort head advances to E03 within the reconcile pass. (c) Mixed pack + per-episode (rare): pack runs first as one entry; per-episode magnets follow per brief §5.4 ordering (pack covers some, per-episode covers gaps). Currently dispatchStreamBulkGroup branches at the top by plan.mode — per-episode and pack are mutually exclusive within a single bulk; mixed mode would require Phase 0/3 plan-builder changes which are out of scope for V2 Phase 2. **Phase 3 (per-episode download progress chips on StreamDetailView's episode list rows) ships next on Hemanth smoke close.** Per /superpowers:requesting-code-review self-review: cohortMaybeAdvance walks group.items[] in stored order which is built episode-order by Phase 0's plan builder; idempotent (return-after-first-promotion + slot-occupancy short-circuit); resumeTorrent is itself idempotent (libtorrent torrent_handle::resume is no-op on already-running handles); mark/publish/onFileRenamed/onFileRenameFailed hooks all collect affectedGroups via the existing groupChanged-tracking pattern, no new walks added; reconcileStreamBulkGroups end-of-function advance fires AFTER the existing reconcile classification pass so it sees libtorrent-truth-aware state; no new public API surface (both methods private); no new persistence file (cohort state implicit in m_streamBulkGroups + libtorrent's own resume.fastresume). Per /security-review: dispatch payload unchanged from Phase 1 (selectedItemKeys filter still gates magnet URI ingress); no new external network surface; resume/pause calls already existed in TorrentClient public API; no priv-elevation; persisted state-machine change reuses existing kStatePending semantic which has no security implications. **CARRY-FORWARD:** 21 prior RTCs (Phase 1 through Phase 8 + STREAM_SUBTITLES_NO_ADDON + AUDIO_HOT_DEVICE_REROUTE + V2 Phase 1) sit unswept since 0584885; my V2 Phase 2 edits sit on top of the working-tree state from those prior RTCs. Files touched this RTC: TorrentClient.{h,cpp} only (already in Phase 6/8 dirty list — Codex's prior edits + my Phase 2 cohort logic co-mingle). When Agent 0 sweeps, attribution will need diff-blob comparison across the chain. Flagged for Agent 0; not blocking.] | Skills invoked: [/simplify, /build-verify, /superpowers:verification-before-completion, /superpowers:requesting-code-review, /security-review] | files: src/core/torrent/TorrentClient.h, src/core/torrent/TorrentClient.cpp, agents/chat.md

READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 4, STREAM_BULK_DOWNLOAD_V2 Phase 3 + RESCUE_DIALOG_KILL 2026-05-10 ~19:53pm — Phase 3 of the V2 arc (per-episode download progress in StreamDetailView's Status column) + Hemanth-direct ask to suppress the StreamRescueProgressDialog popup ("make sure I don't see this notification anymore"). Phase 3 closes the V2 trilogy (Phase 1 picker + Phase 2 cohort scheduler shipped same wake; Phase 3 = visible per-episode download chips). Migration popup kill bundled because (a) Hemanth flagged it together with Phase 3 ask, (b) root-cause was a tight 4-line fix, (c) closing both at once minimizes RTC count for the sweep backlog. **(A) RESCUE_DIALOG_KILL — root-cause analysis + fix.** Hemanth's screenshot showed the popup at 100% with "Done. Added 0 shows and 0 episodes to Stream library. 0 shows could not be matched to Cinemeta and remain in Videos." — Close button required. /superpowers:systematic-debugging Phase 1-2 traced: StreamRescueScanner.cpp line 287 gates the migrationVersion=1 pin behind `if (!m_cancelled.load() && anyShowProcessed && m_metaStore)`. The `anyShowProcessed` requirement means the migration RE-FIRES on every Tankoban launch when Videos has no series-shaped folders OR when no shows match Cinemeta — exactly Hemanth's case ("0 shows" = `anyShowProcessed=false`). The pin never landed, so MainWindow's storedVersion>=1 gate at line 261 stayed open every launch, so the modal popup re-appeared repeatedly. **Fix shape**: 2 surgical edits, no schema change. (1) src/core/stream/StreamRescueScanner.cpp:287 — drop `anyShowProcessed &&` from the pin condition. A non-cancelled walk that reached this line is a successful "nothing to migrate" outcome; persisting the pin honors that semantic. Stats fields preserved as-is (showsMatched/Unmatched/etc. still recorded — telemetry value unchanged). (2) src/ui/MainWindow.cpp:284-294 — removed the StreamRescueProgressDialog construction + show + WA_DeleteOnClose attribute + parent destroy chain. Replaced with silent-run pattern: scanner runs in background, deleteLater fires on `complete` signal. Removed orphan `#include "dialogs/StreamRescueProgressDialog.h"` from MainWindow.cpp:17. Walks items[] checking each state against {Orphaned, Cancelled, Failed, MissingSource, MetadataFailed, PublishFailed} (cleanup states — no live torrent data, no published metadata to preserve). If every item matches: m_streamBulkGroups.remove(groupId) + saveStreamBulkGroups() + return. 8-line rationale comment names the Hemanth ask + cites the line-932 no-op trap that previously made all-terminal groups un-removable. PUBLISHED items are intentionally NOT in the cleanup-state set — groups with any Published items keep their JSON record for user-history purposes (per the existing allPublished branch at line 893 + dialog wording at 2230). **Combined Hemanth-facing flow**: (i) Daredevil S01 detail view in Stream → Status column for episodes 6-9 no longer shows "Failed" (Fix 1). (ii) Tankorent → Daredevil S01 group now renders as a STREAM-chip row even though it's all-Cancelled (Fix 2). (iii) Right-click on the row → "Cancel group..." → confirm dialog → group disappears from Tankorent + JSON record cleared (Fix 3). READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 4, STREAM_BULK_DOWNLOAD_V2 sequential=false hotfix 2026-05-11 ~00:05am — Single-line speed fix for V2 bulk dispatch. Hemanth flagged 2026-05-11: Daredevil S02 bulk dispatch crawling at 4.2 KB/s on a 6.3 GB pack (1 peer, 0 seeds), with verbatim baseline "if I put the same torrent manually individually, it would be downloading at 5-10 mbps." /superpowers:systematic-debugging Phase 1-4 traced: (Phase 1 evidence) screenshot showed parent pack at 4.2 KB/s + 4 child episode-rows all "Downloading" (file-priority-masked, ETA 82h-1064h); same-codebase comparison points all use sequential=false: TankoLibraryPage.cpp:1555 (book downloads, comment "audiobooks don't need sequential; listen-in-order done by player"); TankorentPage.cpp:2342 (manual Tankorent dispatch path). (Phase 2 hypothesis) bulk dispatch's `config.sequential = true` at TorrentClient.cpp:707 forces libtorrent into strict piece-order which starves peer-piece matching in low-seed swarms — the one available peer rarely has the next-in-order piece, so the engine waits; rarest-first (libtorrent default) would have grabbed whatever the peer offered. (Phase 3 root cause) `config.sequential = true` was a streaming-mode artifact carried over from initial Phase 5 dispatch authoring. V2 bulk semantic per Hemanth-locked brief: "download to library, then play" — the user opens completed files from the Stream library AFTER download finishes, NOT while in flight. Stream MODE's streaming path is handled by the stream-server (perpetus) outside libtorrent; libtorrent's per-torrent sequential setting was load-bearing for nothing in this dispatch path. (Phase 4 fix) flipped to false at TorrentClient.cpp:707 with 14-line rationale comment naming Hemanth's verbatim ask + cross-references to TankoLibraryPage + TankorentPage same-codebase precedents + the swarm-reality caveat. **Honest scope:** this removes the ARTIFICIAL throttle (sequential mode) but does NOT change swarm reality. The specific Daredevil S02 pack with 0 seeds + 1 peer will still be slow because the bandwidth literally isn't there. The fix will produce visible speedups on healthy swarms; this pack might stay slow for swarm-health reasons. **Verification:** stop-tankoban.ps1 clean (3 PIDs); build_check.bat BUILD OK first try (single-file edit, single-line code change + comment block). NO build_and_run smoke this RTC — the change is config-only (config.sequential field flow into existing AddTorrentConfig path, no new code), and the user-visible smoke (faster downloads on healthy swarms) requires Hemanth to dispatch a fresh bulk on a show with seeders. **HEMANTH-FACING smoke checklist:** (1) Stop Tankoban, build_and_run.bat to pick up the new binary. (2) **Critical check:** Cancel the existing Daredevil S02 bulk via Tankorent → Cancel group (now works post-prior-RTC fix). (3) Re-dispatch the same Daredevil S02 bulk through Stream → Download Season. (4) Compare download speeds in Tankorent. Pre-fix: 4 KB/s. Post-fix: should match what you'd expect from the swarm — if the swarm is genuinely 1-peer-zero-seed, the fix only saves you the sequential-mode penalty (modest improvement). If you pick a healthier show (e.g., a popular series with many seeders), the fix should produce a dramatic difference vs the prior sequential-mode behavior. (5) **Comparison check:** add the SAME magnet manually via Tankorent's Add URL dialog (different torrent record, full-throttle). Download speeds should now be COMPARABLE between bulk-dispatch and manual-add for the same magnet (both rarest-first now, no other config divergence). Pre-fix the gap would have been obvious; post-fix they should match. **Edge cases worth poking:** (a) Pack-mode bulk (single magnet, file priorities): rarest-first now applies WITHIN the pack. The user gets pieces of any file in the pack as the peer offers them, not strict file-order. Files complete out-of-order. For V2's "download then play" semantic this is fine — the user waits till all files Published before playing. (b) Per-episode bulk (N magnets, cohort scheduler): each individual magnet now gets rarest-first. The cohort scheduler still serializes — only one magnet active at a time — but that one magnet downloads at full rarest-first speed. (c) Sequential mode still applies on the Stream-mode streaming path (handled by stream-server, separate from this dispatch). This fix doesn't touch Stream-mode play-while-streaming behavior. Per /superpowers:requesting-code-review self-review: single-line config change, 14-line comment, no new code paths, no schema change, no signal change; identical to the same-codebase pattern at two other call sites; risk surface is "user wanted strict-order pieces for bulk" which is not V2's stated intent. Per /security-review: no new external surface; libtorrent's rarest-first is the upstream default piece-picker behavior; no privilege change. **CARRY-FORWARD:** 24 prior RTCs unswept since 0584885 (V2 Phase 1+2+3 + dialog kill + Cancelled-group hotfix bundle + this sequential hotfix + 19 prior). Single-file touch this RTC; minimal carry-forward complexity for Agent 0 sweep.] | Skills invoked: [/superpowers:systematic-debugging, /simplify, /build-verify, /superpowers:verification-before-completion, /superpowers:requesting-code-review, /security-review] | files: src/core/torrent/TorrentClient.cpp, agents/chat.md

READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 4, STREAM_BULK_DOWNLOAD_V2 startTorrent + upload_mode hotfix 2026-05-11 ~10:50am — root-cause fix for Hemanth's "torrent downloads are not connecting to any peers" report 2026-05-11. /superpowers:systematic-debugging Phase 1-4 traced the per-episode Phase 2 dispatch chain: (Phase 1 evidence) Hemanth's stream_bulk_groups.json showed correct cohort state (E05=Downloading + E06/E07/E08=Pending) yet Tankorent UI rendered all 4 as Downloading; global session bandwidth was 8.4 KB/s, parent torrent 835 B/s, total peers across the 4 = 1 (one peer connected to E05's swarm). Same Tankoban session had Community.S01 seeding with 15 peers — the engine itself was healthy. (Phase 2 hypothesis) record state diverged from libtorrent ground truth. (Phase 3 root cause) traced the dispatch chain hop-by-hop: (a) `resolveMetadata()` calls `m_engine->addMagnet(magnetUri, resolve_tmp, paused=true)` but `TorrentEngine::addMagnet` at line 624 explicitly does NOT pause libtorrent — comment says "Don't set paused flag — torrent needs to be active to resolve metadata", and sets `upload_mode` flag instead (libtorrent state that allows upload-only, blocks meaningful download). (b) `startDownload()` at line 1530 had the shape `if (!config.startPaused) m_engine->startTorrent(...)` — when V2 Phase 2 set startPaused=true on per-episode cohort items, **startTorrent never ran**. (c) `startTorrent` (TorrentEngine.cpp:804) is what does the load-bearing setup: unsets `upload_mode` at line 811, moves storage from resolve_tmp → destination at line 814, calls `resume()` at line 819. (d) Net result: every V2 Phase 2 per-episode dispatched magnet stayed in resolve_tmp/ with upload_mode set; even when cohortMaybeAdvance later called resumeTorrent(head) to "wake" the cohort head, resumeTorrent only does `handle.resume()` which is a no-op because the torrent was never paused in libtorrent. upload_mode stayed set. Save path stayed wrong. Peer discovery + piece requests crippled. All 4 magnets ran concurrently in this broken state, splitting the trickle of peer-connection-attempts 4 ways, hence "not connecting to peers" + sub-KB/s speeds. **Two-part fix (TorrentClient.cpp, single file):** **(1) Dispatch path fix.** startDownload at line 1530 changed from `if (!config.startPaused) startTorrent(...)` to always-call-startTorrent + optionally-pause-immediately-after. New shape: `m_engine->startTorrent(infoHash, config.destinationPath); if (config.startPaused) m_engine->pauseTorrent(infoHash);`. 13-line rationale comment cites the upload_mode + save-path trap and Phase 2's symptom chain. Effect on each caller: (a) per-episode bulk dispatch (V2 Phase 2): non-head items now go startTorrent → pauseTorrent → properly paused at staging path with upload_mode cleared; head goes startTorrent → not paused → runs at full peer discovery. (b) pack bulk dispatch: startPaused=false → startTorrent → not paused → unchanged behavior. (c) TankorentPage manual-add with startImmediately=false (line 2343 startPaused=true): previously stuck in resolve_tmp with upload_mode; now properly at destPath, paused, upload_mode cleared. Pre-existing bug fixed as side benefit. (d) TankorentPage manual-add with startImmediately=true: no behavior change. **(2) Reconcile-time heal pass.** Added at end of `reconcileStreamBulkGroups` (after the existing classification loop, before cohortMaybeAdvanceAll). Walks every non-terminal item in every group with non-empty infoHash. For each: calls `m_engine->startTorrent(infoHash, group.stagingPath)` (idempotent — only moves storage if save path differs, always unsets upload_mode); then if item.state == "Pending", immediately calls `pauseTorrent(infoHash)` to enforce the cohort scheduler's slot semantic. 13-line rationale comment cites Hemanth's report + the dispatch-chain trap. Effect: at next Tankoban launch, Hemanth's stuck Daredevil S02 group items get unstuck — E05 (Downloading record) gets startTorrent run on it → upload_mode cleared + storage moved from resolve_tmp to .tankoban-partial/<groupId>/ + resumed; E06/E07/E08 (Pending records) get same setup then immediately re-paused. Cohort scheduler then drives normal one-at-a-time progress. **Verification:** stop-tankoban.ps1 clean (3 PIDs); build_check.bat BUILD OK first try (TorrentClient.cpp single-file edit, 2 surgical changes). NO build_and_run smoke this RTC — the fixes are state-machine logic in the libtorrent integration layer and would require a fresh dispatch + sustained download to validate end-to-end peer behavior (Hemanth's gate). **HEMANTH-FACING smoke checklist:** **(1) Critical setup:** stop Tankoban, build_and_run.bat to pick up the new binary. **(2) The startup heal:** when Tankoban launches, the reconcile pass fires automatically on the existing Daredevil S02 group. E05 (Downloading record) gets unstuck: upload_mode cleared, storage moved from resolve_tmp to .tankoban-partial/<groupId>/. E06/E07/E08 get same setup + immediately re-paused. **(3) Verify in Tankorent:** open Tankorent → Daredevil S02 group should now show E05 as the ONLY actively-downloading row. E06/E07/E08 should show as Paused. Peer count + download speed on E05 should ramp up over 30-90s as DHT finds peers properly (no more upload_mode blocking). **(4) Expected speed delta:** if the swarm has seeders, E05 should reach KB/s → MB/s range (vs the prior 835 B/s with 4 concurrent broken torrents). The Daredevil S02 swarm specifically might still be thin (0 seeds, low peer count) — that's swarm reality not engine state. (5) **Cohort advance smoke:** wait for E05 to download substantially or all the way; verify E06 auto-resumes when E05 enters Publishing/Published; verify only ONE item is ever actively running at a time. (6) **Regression: pack mode** — dispatch a fresh bulk on a different show that lands as a pack source; verify the pack starts immediately (startPaused=false unchanged behavior). (7) **Regression: manual Tankorent add with "Don't start"** — if you use the Add URL dialog with the "start immediately" toggle off, the torrent should appear paused at the chosen destination (not at resolve_tmp). Pre-fix it would have been stuck at resolve_tmp; post-fix it's correctly at destination. **Edge cases:** (a) reconcile pass runs once per Tankoban launch. If user manually pauses a Downloading item via Tankorent UI mid-session, scheduler does NOT re-resume (user-is-god per brief §7.g — cohortMaybeAdvance's "slot occupied" check counts Downloading items; manually-paused items don't count as Downloading). READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 3 (Codex), AUDIO_HOT_DEVICE_REROUTE_FIX2]: Fix default-device reroute rebinding to stale PortAudio cached device. Added audio_device_watcher::resolve_current_wasapi_default_device_index(), which calls live IMMDeviceEnumerator::GetDefaultAudioEndpoint(eRender,eMultimedia), reads IMMDevice::GetId, then iterates PortAudio WASAPI devices and matches each device via PaWasapi_GetIMMDevice + IMMDevice::GetId. PortAudio DOES expose the needed per-device IMMDevice pointer in pa_win_wasapi.h; no friendly-name fallback was needed for the primary match key. audio_decoder.cpp now uses this live endpoint-ID resolver for reroute rebuilds and lazy opens; rebuild_for_new_default no longer reads PaHostApiInfo::defaultOutputDevice. Successful rebuild logs AVSYNC_DIAG audio_reroute_complete with dev_idx, name, endpoint_id, and match method. Failure paths log AVSYNC_DIAG audio_reroute_failed and keep the existing stale stream active until Pa_WriteStream itself reports device loss, instead of silently failing or tearing audio down early. main.cpp prewarm now uses the same live resolver, with PortAudio cached default only as a logged fallback, and audio_device_watcher::init() moved to immediately after Pa_Initialize and before Bluetooth prewarm so watcher registration is visible even if prewarm is slow. build_and_run.bat now sets TANKOBAN_SIDECAR_DEBUG=1 so sidecar stderr/AVSYNC_DIAG lines are captured. Open questions answered: (1) PortAudio exposes PaWasapi_GetIMMDevice, not a raw endpoint string, but that is enough for exact endpoint-ID matching through IMMDevice::GetId. (2) Prewarm uses a short-lived COM apartment inside the resolver helper; watcher registration is also moved before prewarm for lifecycle/validation, without depending on watcher global state for resolution. (3) If endpoint lookup or match/open/start fails, fallback is explicit logging plus continuing on the old active stream, preserving pre-Fix-1 behavior until the stream errors. READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 3 (Codex), AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE_RETRY hotfix]: Fixed the "Invalid sample rate" local-playback failure that left video stuck on a frame with no audio clock. Evidence from sidecar_debug_live.log before the fix: `[SubsPlease] One Piece - 1161 (1080p) [9BEAE717].mkv` opened with AAC in_rate=44100/out_rate=44100, lazy output dev_idx=11 `Mi TV (Intel(R) Display Audio)`, then `AudioDecoder: Pa_OpenStream failed: Invalid sample rate`; video then emitted repeated `AVSYNC_DIAG video_clock_wait_TIMEOUT (2s, no audio clock)` and only advanced as isolated frames. Fix: audio_decoder.cpp now wraps swresample setup in a reusable init_resampler(out_rate) helper; when lazy Pa_OpenStream returns paInvalidSampleRate, it reads PaDeviceInfo::defaultSampleRate for the selected PortAudio output device, logs `AVSYNC_DIAG audio_sample_rate_retry` with rejected_rate/retry_rate/dev_idx/name, rebuilds the resampler to that retry rate, and retries Pa_OpenStream once before decoding/writing any samples. This keeps output buffers at the same rate as the stream actually opened. Verification: native_sidecar/build.ps1 passed; build_check.bat returned BUILD OK; git diff --check clean aside from expected LF-to-CRLF warnings; launched Tankoban via build_and_run.bat with cleared sidecar_debug_live.log; tankoctl ping returned schema=tankoban.dev.v1; tankoctl play-file on the exact failing One Piece MKV succeeded; tankoctl get-player reported firstFrameSeen=true, openPending=false, sidecarRetryCount=0, streamStalled=false; current log shows `AudioDecoder: codec=aac in_rate=44100 out_rate=44100`, `audio_lazy_cached_default ... dev_idx=11 name='Mi TV (Intel(R) Display Audio)'`, `audio_sample_rate_retry ... rejected_rate=44100 retry_rate=48000`, `audio_pa_open_done +238ms`, `audio_ready_signal +241ms`, and `VideoDecoder: first frame`. Rule 17 cleanup ran. NOTE: the same smoke also produced a separate later `audio_reroute_failed` line after Windows default changed to an endpoint PortAudio did not have in its initialized WASAPI device list (`no PortAudio WASAPI device matched live endpoint id ... candidates=2`). That is not the sample-rate/stuck-frame bug; it is remaining evidence that hot-plug reroute can still fail when the new endpoint was not present in PortAudio's Pa_Initialize-time device list. | Skills invoked: [/superpowers:systematic-debugging, /simplify, /build-verify, /superpowers:verification-before-completion, /superpowers:requesting-code-review, /security-review] | files: native_sidecar/src/audio_decoder.cpp, agents/chat.md

(Phase 2 hypothesis) record state diverged from cohort state — items appeared "Pending" in the cohort store but the underlying libtorrent session held NO live handle for E06/E07/E08, just persisted zombie m_records entries. (Phase 3 root cause) traced the failure chain: (a) Daredevil S02 bulk dispatched in a Tankoban session BEFORE my 2026-05-11 10:50am startTorrent + upload_mode hotfix; pre-fix per-episode magnets resolved metadata but stuck in upload_mode at resolve_tmp/ without ever firing save_resume_data. (b) Tankoban shut down; no .fastresume files were written for E06/E07/E08 — they exist ONLY as m_records entries. (c) Subsequent launches: boot loop at TorrentClient.cpp:370-388 found no .fastresume → marked records state="error". (d) reconcileStreamBulkGroups orphan-detection only flips items to Orphaned when m_records lacks the record entirely, NOT when the record exists in error state — coverage gap. (e) restartStreamBulkGroup's forceRecheck branch ran but was a no-op: forceRecheck targets a libtorrent handle, but the handle was never added (addFromResume returned empty). (f) retryStreamBulkGroupFailedItems didn't trigger because its gate matches MissingSource/MetadataFailed/Pending-without-hash; Pending-WITH-orphan-hash didn't match. (Phase 4 implementation) extended restartStreamBulkGroup at TorrentClient.cpp:1167+ to distinguish orphan-by-error from transient-error via TWO snapshots: persistedStates (from listActive, m_records-backed) + liveHashes (QSet from m_engine->allStatuses, returns ONLY hashes with valid handles in the libtorrent session). Distinction rule: persisted="error" + live-handle-present → transient libtorrent error → forceRecheck via the public wrapper (existing path preserved); persisted="error" + live-handle-ABSENT → orphan-by-missing-resume → demote item to MissingSource + clear infoHash/torrentKey/fileIndex/fileKey + record orphan for cleanup. After items[] commit, deleteTorrent(orphanHash, false) on each cleans the zombie m_records entry (safe with no live handle: TorrentEngine::removeTorrent early-returns if m_records.find returns end; QFile::remove on missing .fastresume returns false harmlessly). Finally if any orphans recovered, retryStreamBulkGroupFailedItems(groupId) fires the existing source-pick-rerun plumbing — StreamPage rebuilds the bulk plan, reruns BulkSourceCollector + buildBulkSelection, dispatches fresh magnets via Phase 5 group path. Fresh magnets land paused via the cohort scheduler; if existing head (E05) is still Downloading, slot stays occupied and new dispatches wait their turn. CRITICAL ordering: the item's infoHash field is CLEARED before the deleteTorrent call because deleteTorrent invokes markStreamBulkItemsForTorrent which loops items by infoHash and would otherwise overwrite my just-demoted MissingSource state with Cancelled. **Verification:** stop-tankoban.ps1 clean kill (1 stremio-runtime PID); build_check.bat BUILD OK first try; cohort scheduler interaction unchanged for the non-orphan path (cohortMaybeAdvance still fires when no orphans recovered). **HEMANTH SMOKE GATE:** (1) Launch Tankoban via build_and_run.bat. (4) Within ~10-30s (depending on addon response), the source picker should resolve fresh magnets; new torrents get added paused via the cohort scheduler. (5) Tankorent shows the 3 newly-dispatched torrents in Paused state with fresh infoHashes. (6) When E05 completes/publishes, cohort head advances to E06 → E06 starts downloading → E07 → E08. (7) If source picker fails (no peers, addon errors) items stay MissingSource — click Retry failed or Restart group again. **Edge case audit:** (a) E05 is state="downloading" with valid live handle → falls through both orphan + transient branches → reset-to-Pending fires if not already Pending → cohort takes over. Verified clean per torrents.json. (b) Pack-mode bulks: orphan detection is hash-by-hash; pack items share one infoHash so if it's orphaned all items demote together and retry fires a fresh pack source-pick. (c) Mixed Published + orphan groups: Published items skip the loop entirely (terminal-success guard), only non-Published items get orphan/transient/reset treatment — Published metadata preserved. Per /security-review: no new external surface; reuses TorrentClient's existing public surface (forceRecheck, deleteTorrent, retryStreamBulkGroupFailedItems); m_engine->allStatuses() is existing public engine query; orphan cleanup only deletes zombie m_records entries we own. **CARRY-FORWARD:** 26 prior RTCs unswept since 0584885. READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 4, TANKORENT_BULK_COHORT_PERSISTENCE_FIX 2026-05-11 ~7:12pm — root-cause fix for Hemanth's "but it's not sequentially downloading :(" report after the prior orphan-recovery RTC. /superpowers:systematic-debugging Phase 1-4: (Phase 1 evidence) live torrents.json showed all 4 fresh-dispatched Daredevil S02 magnets (addedAt timestamps clustered ~5pm) had m_records.state="metadata_ready" — yet stream_bulk_groups.json cohort state showed E05="Downloading" + E06/E07/E08="Pending" (correctly set by the retry-dispatch path). The two-store divergence was the diagnostic signal. (Phase 2 hypothesis) m_records.state was being clobbered AFTER startDownload wrote the correct paused/downloading state. (Phase 3 root cause) traced onMetadataReady at TorrentClient.cpp:2014 — the signal handler unconditionally overwrites rec["state"] to "metadata_ready" whenever libtorrent's metadata_received_alert fires. After the 2026-05-11 startTorrent+upload_mode hotfix, startDownload writes state EXPLICITLY to "paused" or "downloading" via the config.startPaused branch at line 1707 — BEFORE libtorrent fires metadata_received_alert (which takes seconds for fresh magnets). The late-firing alert then clobbered the explicit state with "metadata_ready", with two devastating downstream consequences: (a) on NEXT boot, the addFromResume loop at line 380 reads m_records.state to pick its shouldPause flag via `shouldPause = (state == "paused")` — "metadata_ready" doesn't match "paused" → shouldPause=false → ALL cohort items add UNPAUSED in libtorrent → not sequential. (b) the heal pass + cohortMaybeAdvanceAll at the tail of reconcileStreamBulkGroups were both NO-OPS at boot because the order in the ctor was reconcileStreamBulkGroups (line 366) BEFORE the addFromResume loop (line 369-388) — m_engine had zero libtorrent handles when the heal pass + cohort advance fired, so startTorrent/pauseTorrent/resumeTorrent all early-returned on `!handle.is_valid()`. The heal pass that was SUPPOSED to enforce cohort pause/resume invariants on boot literally did nothing. (Phase 4 implementation) three coordinated edits in TorrentClient.cpp: **(1) onMetadataReady at line 2014:** removed the `rec["state"] = "metadata_ready"` write. Records are only ever created by startDownload (line 1707 — the sole writer that creates an entry from scratch); onMetadataReady's `if (m_records.contains)` check means it only fires when a record ALREADY exists, and that record's state was already set explicitly by startDownload. Name update is preserved (load-bearing for UI display + history). 14-line rationale comment cites the late-alert clobber + addFromResume gate. **(2) Reorder addFromResume loop BEFORE reconcileStreamBulkGroups in ctor:** the loop at lines 369-388 was moved up to run immediately after m_engine->start(); reconcileStreamBulkGroups now runs AFTER addFromResume has populated m_engine with handles. This ensures the heal pass at reconcile's tail + cohortMaybeAdvanceAll both operate on real libtorrent handles instead of being no-op walks. retryStreamBulkPublishing still runs after both. 12-line rationale comment cites the no-handle trap + chain-of-dependencies for heal pass effectiveness. **(3) Heal pass wrappers (lines 645-661):** replaced bare `m_engine->pauseTorrent(infoHash)` with public `pauseTorrent(infoHash)` wrapper so m_records.state syncs to "paused" + torrentUpdated signal fires. READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 4, TANKORENT_FILES_TAB_TREE_HIERARCHY 2026-05-11 ~7:25pm — fixes Hemanth's Tankorent "Torrent Properties → Files" tab rendering for multi-level torrents (screenshot 2026-05-11 ~18:45 showed the Community.S01-S06.COMPLETE.1080p.BluRay.DD5.1.With.Commentary.x265-POIASD pack as ~15 visually-identical rows; reference screenshot of Deluge on a One Pace pack showed the correct expandable tree with root → Season subfolders → episodes). 4B-domain work executed via subagent-driven dispatch from Agent 4's session per Hemanth's option-2 pick after /superpowers:writing-plans authored the plan at docs/superpowers/plans/2026-05-11-tankorent-files-tab-tree-hierarchy.md. **Root cause** (TorrentFilesTab.cpp:146-202 pre-fix): populateTree() did a single-level fullPath.lastIndexOf('/') split, keyed folder rows by the entire path-minus-basename string, AND set the folder row's display text to that same cumulative-prefix string. So for `Pack/Season 1/episode.mkv` paths the code created ONE folder per unique `Pack/Season N` prefix (no shared `Pack/` parent), labeled each "Pack/Season N", and on long torrent-root names every folder label visually truncated to the same prefix. **Fix shape** (single file pair, no new files, no CMakeLists touch): (1) TorrentFilesTab.h gains <QHash>/<QList> includes, a FolderRow struct (pathKey, totalSize, item, descendantFileIndexes), a QHash<QString, FolderRow> m_folders member alongside m_rows, and a cascadePriorityToDescendants(QTreeWidgetItem*, int) declaration. (2) setInfoHash clears m_folders alongside m_rows. (3) populateTree rewritten end-to-end: splits each fullPath via fullPath.split('/', Qt::SkipEmptyParts), walks parts.size()-1 components creating/looking-up folder items by cumulative path key, labels each folder with ONLY the trailing segment (`Season 1`, not `Pack/Season 1`) per setText(0, segment), accumulates the leaf's size + index into every ancestor's totalSize / descendantFileIndexes, then emits the leaf row with the existing combo-box priority widget; Pass 2 paints each folder's aggregate Size (humanSize of totalSize) and initial size-weighted-average Progress. (4) refresh() extended with a folder-progress recompute pass: builds a QHash<int,double> progressByIdx during the leaf pass, then a folder loop computes size-weighted-average across each folder's descendantFileIndexes — folder Progress % now ticks live at 1Hz. (5) cascadePriorityToDescendants defined at EOF: depth-first walk via childCount(), at leaves (childCount==0) drives the row's combo via setCurrentIndex; replaces the prior single-level child-iteration in onTreeContextMenu's setPriority lambda's `else` branch with a single call so multi-level folder right-click → priority cascades to ALL descendants. **Code-review fix cycle** (catch by reviewer subagent, fixes by same implementer subagent): Important #1 — Pass 2 originally re-parsed the formatted "%.1f%" leaf-percent text to back out a double (lossy + brittle); fixed by capturing raw `progress` doubles into a local QHash<int,double> leafProgress during Pass 1 and consuming it directly in Pass 2. Important #2 — header comment on cascadePriorityToDescendants stated "Returns true if at least one leaf priority was changed" but the function is void; rewrote comment to describe the actual void recursive contract + that the caller is responsible for invoking writePrioritiesToEngine(). Both fixes ~one-line each in already-touched regions. **Verification:** stop-tankoban.ps1 clean (1 stremio-runtime PID killed); build_check.bat BUILD OK on the post-fix-cycle state. No smoke driven by me — the Files-tab is read-mostly UI and requires Hemanth to open the dialog on a real multi-level torrent. **HEMANTH SMOKE GATE:** (1) Rebuild + relaunch via build_and_run.bat to pick up the new binary. (2) Tankorent → double-click any season-pack torrent with a `Root/Season N/episode.mkv` structure (e.g. the Community pack from the bug screenshot). (3) Click the Files tab. (4) Verify ONE top-level row shows just the torrent root folder name (`Community.S01-S06.COMPLETE.1080p.BluRay.DD5.1.With.Commentary.x265-POIASD`), expanded by default, with `Season 1`, `Season 2`, ... children labeled with just their leaf names (no `Pack/` prefix). Underneath each Season, individual episode files with basenames only. (5) Folder rows show aggregate Size in column 1 (sum of descendant leaves) and aggregate Progress % in column 2 (size-weighted average). On a downloading torrent the Progress % updates each second. (6) Right-click a Season folder → "Skip" → ALL episodes under that Season flip to Skip in their per-row dropdowns. Right-click the root folder → "Normal" → everything resets. Right-click a single leaf → "Maximum" → only that one row's dropdown flips. (7) Single-level torrents (no root folder, e.g. movie torrents with one .mkv) keep rendering correctly — flat single-row case still works because parts.size()-1 == 0 means zero folder iterations, leaf goes straight to m_tree. **Out-of-scope (confirmed not touched):** torrentFiles JSON contract on TorrentEngine, General/Trackers/Peers tabs, TorrentPropertiesWidget, CMakeLists.txt, QSS styling, icons, sort order. **File-level delta:** TorrentFilesTab.h 59→83 lines (+24); TorrentFilesTab.cpp 338→426 lines (+88 net). Per /superpowers:requesting-code-review (covered by two-stage subagent reviews this dispatch — spec-compliance reviewer + code-quality reviewer + post-fix re-verification by controller); per /security-review: no new external surface — UI-only tree render of pre-existing TorrentEngine::torrentFiles JSON data, no new IPC, no new persistence, no new attack surface; the rename-context-menu path was NOT touched (still calls existing TorrentClient.engine.renameFile). **CARRY-FORWARD:** 28 prior RTCs unswept since 0584885 (this is #29). MB) — those are LEAF rows. Zero folder rows visible. Either (a) the Community torrent really had no subfolders, or (b) my path split was finding zero separators. (Phase 3 root cause) checked the .fastresume bencoded blob at LOCALAPPDATA/Tankoban/data/torrent_cache/resume/4064457a6e48fd04e1ac57875b182d952757bd93.fastresume — the bencoded path list clearly shows TWO-level folder structure: `Community.S01.1080p.BluRay.DD5.1.With.Commentary.x265-POIASD` (season folder) followed by `Community.S01E01.Pilot.1080p.BluRay.DD5.1.x265-POIASD.mkv` (episode file). The torrent IS multi-level. So the split was finding zero separators. Checked libtorrent source at C:/tools/libtorrent-source/src/file_storage.cpp:58-62 — `TORRENT_SEPARATOR` is defined as `'\'` on TORRENT_WINDOWS (and `'/'` elsewhere). **The plan I authored explicitly stated "libtorrent file_path(i) returns POSIX-separator paths regardless of host OS" — that was wrong on Windows.** The implementer faithfully implemented `fullPath.split('/', Qt::SkipEmptyParts)` per the plan, which found zero forward slashes in real Windows paths and produced `parts.size() == 1`; the folder loop `for (p = 0; p < parts.size() - 1; ++p)` ran zero iterations; every leaf hit `m_tree` directly. Pre-fix `lastIndexOf('/')` had the SAME bug — `-1` lookup → empty dir → flat row. So the rendering bug was identical before-and-after the prior RTC's "fix"; the prior fix was a no-op on Windows. (Phase 4 fix) one surgical edit + one include: src/ui/pages/tankorent/TorrentFilesTab.cpp gains `#include <QRegularExpression>` and the path-split now uses `static const QRegularExpression kPathSep(R"([\/])");` + `fullPath.split(kPathSep, Qt::SkipEmptyParts)` so it splits on BOTH `\` and `/` defensively. The cumulative-key construction still uses `/` (canonical key; doesn't need to match the source separator since it's only used for lookup). (3) Expected NOW: top-level row labeled just `Community.S01-S06.COMPLETE.1080p.BluRay.DD5.1.With.Commentary.x265-POIASD` (the outer root folder, expanded), under it 6 season-folder rows labeled `Community.S01.1080p.BluRay.DD5.1.With.Commentary.x265-POIASD` / `Community.S02.1080p.BluRay.DD5.1.With.Commentary.x265-POIASD` / ... etc, and under each season the individual `Community.S0XE0Y.{title}.1080p.BluRay.DD5.1.x265-POIASD.mkv` episode rows with per-row Priority dropdowns. (4) Folder rows show aggregate Size (sum of descendant leaves) and live aggregate Progress %. (5) Right-click a season folder → "Skip" cascades to all episodes underneath; right-click the outer root → "Normal" resets everything. **Memory follow-up:** save feedback_libtorrent_windows_backslash_separator.md so future agents don't replay the audit-claim error. **Per /superpowers:requesting-code-review self-review:** the prior RTC's two-stage subagent review chain passed because both reviewers also implicitly trusted the plan's separator claim — neither cross-referenced libtorrent's actual TORRENT_SEPARATOR definition. READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 4, TANKORENT_BULK_PUBLISH_AUTORETRY_AND_INDEX_REGISTRATION_FIX 2026-05-11 ~9:35pm — closes STREAM_DOWNLOADED_LIBRARY spec Rule C ("Click downloaded episode → auto-play, no source-pick") which Hemanth reported violated for his Daredevil S02 group at 2026-05-11 ~9:24pm. /superpowers:systematic-debugging Phase 1-4 traced TWO compounding defects after the prior absolute-path publish fix landed: (Defect A) `publishStreamBulkItemsForTorrent` at TorrentClient.cpp:1405 gates on `isDownloadingStreamBulkState(state)` (matches Pending+Downloading only); PublishFailed items don't match → publish-retry never auto-fires on boot. retryStreamBulkPublishing's seedingLike branch at TorrentClient.cpp:1483 also gates on isDownloadingStreamBulkState → completedHashesNeedingPublish set excludes PublishFailed → boot-time auto-retry skipped these items entirely. (Defect B) `onFileRenamed` at TorrentClient.cpp:2238-2247 reads `item.value("imdbId")` / `item.value("streamSeason")` / `item.value("episodeNum")` — fields the CURRENT bulk dispatch path NEVER populates (they were intended for a future enrichment that didn't ship). Consequence: the `if (!imdbId.isEmpty() && seasonNum > 0 && episodeNum > 0)` gate at line 2241 ALWAYS FAILED, so StreamDownloadIndex::registerEpisode was SILENTLY SKIPPED on every successful publish. Then StreamDetailView::onEpisodeActivated calls `m_downloadIndex->filePathFor(...)` which returns empty → falls through to source-pick → Sources pane appears. **Spec Rule C is violated regardless of publish success.** Even if Defect A is fixed, Defect B still breaks Rule C — they're two layers of the same chain, both broken. (Phase 4 implementation) three coordinated edits in TorrentClient.cpp: **(1) publishStreamBulkItemsForTorrent gate (line 1405) extended:** accepts PublishFailed in addition to Downloading/Pending. The rest of the function (canonicalPath validation + mkpath + relativePublishTarget (now absolute per the prior hotfix) + state=Publishing + m_engine->renameFile) handles PublishFailed identically to Downloading — a retry-from-scratch. 8-line rationale comment cites the auto-retry chain. **(2) retryStreamBulkPublishing's "queue for publish" branch (line 1483) extended:** now matches `isDownloadingStreamBulkState(state) || state == kStatePublishFailed`. 6-line rationale comment cites the seedingLike gate already confirming bytes-on-disk + cross-references the absolute-path rename fix. Net effect: on every Tankoban boot, any PublishFailed item whose underlying torrent is in seedingLike state (completed/seeding) gets re-queued for publish; the absolute-path rename target should now succeed; state transitions PublishFailed → Publishing → (libtorrent rename) → Published via onFileRenamed. **(3) onFileRenamed StreamDownloadIndex registration (line 2237-2274) — fallback chain:** if imdbId / seasonNum / episodeNum are empty/zero on the item (current bulk dispatch path's state), fall back to: `group.sourceIds.seriesId` for imdbId, `group.sourceIds.season` for seasonNum, and parse episodeNum from the itemKey's `<imdbId>:S<NN>E<NN>` suffix via `itemKey.lastIndexOf('E')` + toInt. All three are guaranteed-present in any group dispatched via Phase 0+ buildBulkPlan. 25-line rationale comment block cites the spec §6.1, the silently-skipped registration root cause, and the fallback chain semantics. **Combined chain effect** for Hemanth's Daredevil S02 group: (a) next Tankoban boot → retryStreamBulkPublishing runs → PublishFailed items detected → publishStreamBulkItemsForTorrent re-fires for each → renameFile with absolute target → libtorrent processes rename → onFileRenamed fires → StreamDownloadIndex::registerEpisode now succeeds (via fallback chain) → tile badge flips to DOWNLOADED + per-episode markers light up in StreamDetailView. (b) user clicks E05 in detail view → onEpisodeActivated → filePathFor(tt18923754, 2, 5) returns the canonical path → file exists check passes → emit playLocalFileFromStreamRequested → MainWindow::onPlayLocalFileFromStreamRequested → m_videoPlayer->openFile(localPath) → video plays directly. NO sources pane. NO boot smoke this RTC — the fix is a 3-edit gate-widening + fallback-chain change with no init-path side-effects beyond what's already exercised by every boot. Combined with the prior 2026-05-11 ~7:42pm absolute-path fix, Hemanth's Daredevil S02 group should auto-recover on next launch with zero manual Retry-failed clicks. **HEMANTH SMOKE GATE:** (1) Stop Tankoban; `build_and_run.bat` to pick up new binary. Per-child rows should transition from "Publish failed" → "Publishing" → "Published" within seconds of boot (libtorrent's rename is sub-second per file). (4) Once all 4 published, the files appear at `<Videos>/Daredevil Born Again (2025–)/Season 02/Daredevil Born Again - S02E{05..08} - <title>.mkv` — verifiable via `ls`. (5) Open Stream → Daredevil: Born Again detail view → episodes 5-8 should show the on-disk download icon (mounted on the # column per StreamDetailView::refreshEpisodeMarkers triggered by StreamDownloadIndex::entriesChanged). (6) **Critical Rule C check:** click episode 5 row → video plays directly via local-file path → Sources pane does NOT appear. Click episode 6/7/8 same behavior. (7) Right-click any of E05-E08 → context menu shows "Show alternate streams" (Rule D escape hatch — opens source-pick on demand). (8) Stream library home view → Daredevil: Born Again tile shows the DOWNLOADED chip (Rule B). **Edge cases:** (a) future bulk dispatches will also benefit: items dispatched via the current path don't populate imdbId/streamSeason/episodeNum, so without this fix every bulk-downloaded episode (across all shows) would have been click-falls-through-to-source-pick. The fallback chain rescues all of them retroactively + going forward. (b) the `if (state != PublishFailed) state must be Downloading/Pending` extension only widens the auto-retry set — it does NOT introduce a way for Published items to revert (Published items don't match either branch). Idempotent — re-running the backfill on a fully-up-to-date index is a no-op except for the cheap filePathFor lookups. (c) Items where the on-disk file got deleted manually are skipped + counted under skippedNoFile — better to leave unregistered than advertise a ghost entry that fails the click-to-play. (d) entriesChanged signal emits on each registerEpisode call; at backfill time only TorrentClient is wired (StreamPage/StreamDetailView wire at MainWindow.cpp:584+), but those subscribers immediately call refreshEpisodeMarkers/refresh in their own setStreamDownloadIndex setters which re-query state — so initial paint shows the backfilled entries. **Per /superpowers:requesting-code-review self-review:** two-method addition + setter behavior change. registerEpisode itself is idempotent + early-exits on bad inputs; backfill walks a bounded set (m_streamBulkGroups by N groups × M items per group, both small). Performance: O(groups × items × QFileInfo::exists) — ~3 syscalls per Published item on Hemanth's machine (~4 items total = ~12 syscalls). Not measurable. **Per /security-review:** no new external surface; reuses StreamDownloadIndex's existing public registerEpisode API; canonical path construction uses QDir::cleanPath which prevents path-traversal even if destinationKey were maliciously crafted (it can't be — it's our own data). ## Agent 4 — STREAM_DOWNLOADS_NETFLIX_OVERHAUL brainstorm CLOSED — 2026-05-12 04:30pm

Brainstorm-only RTC. Spec authored at `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-12-stream-downloads-netflix-overhaul-design.md` per Hemanth's pipeline (brainstorm → THIS → writing-plans → executing-plans, each phase Hemanth-fired separately).

**Headline picks (Hemanth-locked P1-P8):**
- **P1 Primary surface** = inline on StreamDetailView episode list — per-row + per-season-header triggers + controls; NO preflight dialog (V2 Phase 1 dialog formally superseded).
- **P2 Secondary surface** = new sidebar-drawer "Downloads" entry → new `StreamDownloadsPage` (Active section + History section, both grouped-by-show, 90-day history TTL).
- **P3 Sequential scope** = within-show sequential, parallel across shows (Layer 2 Phase 2 cohort scheduler unchanged; no global cap).
- **P4 In-flight tile chip** = text-only "DOWNLOADING" parallel to Layer 3's "DOWNLOADED"; in-flight wins when both apply; user-paused cohort still counts as in-flight (chip flips to DOWNLOADED only on terminal-state).
- **P5 Selection UX** = checkboxes always visible on every row + per-row morphing trigger icon; season header has "Download Season" (queue every row) + "Download Selected (N)" (queue checked subset).
- **P6 Row controls** = single morphing icon `[↓] → [⏸] → [▶] → [✓] / [↻]`; cancel hidden in right-click only.
- **P7 Season header pattern** = parity with rows: single morphing button "Download Season" / "Pause Season" / "Continue Season"; right-click button OR season-combo → "Cancel Season".
- **P8 Cancel semantic** = files deleted on cancel (per-episode cancel deletes that one file; season cancel deletes whole cohort INCLUDING completed episodes); pause preserves files.

**Rule 14 architectural picks (Agent 4):**
- Zero new persistence files. Reuse `stream_bulk_groups.json` (Layer 1) + `stream_downloads.json` (Layer 3 / StreamDownloadIndex) + `stream_bulk_canonical_maps.json`. Extend `pruneTerminalStreamBulkGroups` TTL from 7d → 90d so the History section reads it directly. Two new TorrentClient read APIs (`streamBulkGroupsSnapshot()` + `imdbHasActiveCohort()`) + one new signal (`streamBulkGroupsChanged`).
- Failure handling: existing cohort-state machinery drives per-row `[↻]` retry icon dispatching `retryStreamBulkGroupFailedItems` (extended to accept optional per-item filter). No auto-retry.
- Restart resilience inherited from Layer 1 §6.4 + Layer 2 cohort persistence + Layer 3 lazy validation. No new restart logic.

**Reconciliation with prior layers (full table in spec §3):**
- **Layer 1 (STREAM_BULK_DOWNLOAD, 2026-05-07 spec, shipped Phases 0-7)** — PRESERVED: source-pick + canonical naming + libtorrent dispatch + `streamGroupId` + canonical-maps JSON. SUPERSEDED: Tankorent group-row representation + Q5 group-level-only granularity. EXTENDED: pruneTerminalStreamBulkGroups TTL.
- **Layer 2 (STREAM_BULK_DOWNLOAD_V2, no formal spec, Phases 1+2+3 + hotfix chain all shipped in chat.md)** — PRESERVED: cohort scheduler (V2 Phase 2) + per-episode Status column (V2 Phase 3). SUPERSEDED: StreamBulkPreflightDialog (V2 Phase 1) — to be deleted in executing-plans. ABSORBED: V2 hotfix chain (BULK_MENU_RESTRUCTURE, GROUP_PARENT_ROW_ALIGNMENT, BULK_ORPHAN_RECOVERY, BULK_COHORT_PERSISTENCE_FIX) — Tankorent UI those targeted goes away; engine-side fixes stay load-bearing.
- **Layer 3 (STREAM_DOWNLOADED_LIBRARY, 2026-05-10 spec, shipped)** — PRESERVED: StreamDownloadIndex + DOWNLOADED chip + click-to-auto-play (Rule C) + right-click "Show alternate streams" (Rule D) + Videos-scanner skip + first-launch rescue + remove-from-library evicts. EXTENDED: §2.2's deferred "in-flight tile visualization" is closed by P4 DOWNLOADING chip; episode-row marker logic now covers in-flight states not just downloaded.
- **2026-05-12 wake's uncommitted hotfix work (STREAM_DOWNLOAD_INDEX_BACKFILL + STREAM_DOWNLOAD_INDEX_WIRING_HOIST + StreamDetailView instrumentation + StreamLibraryLayout chip tidy)** — load-bearing carry-through; lands alongside this overhaul's executing-plans.

**Migration:** none required (Hemanth verbatim "you can delete or undownload all the downloads we've done, so no worries"). Clean-slate cutover: Hemanth manually cancels any in-flight Tankorent stream-bulk via existing UI before the overhaul ships; post-ship surface excludes any remaining stream-grouped rows. No migrationVersion pin, no migration JSON, no progress dialog. Spec §10.

**Scope reductions vs the wake brief's §4 brainstorm topic list:**
- §4.1 (data model layout): zero new persistence files — picked under Rule 14, no Hemanth menu-ing.
- §4.8 (storage path): preserved from Layer 3 Rule F — no change.
- §4.10 (migration from Tankorent grouped rows): MOOT post-Hemanth-clean-slate-OK.
- §4.12 (future extensions): preserved as future-extension flags in §2.3, NOT designed.

**Spec self-review:** placeholder scan clean (no TBDs, no untyped methods); internal consistency reviewed (3 inline fixes applied — clarified dead-code scope on TankorentPage, removed misleading "selected" state row from the row state map, made paused-cohort tile chip rule explicit); scope is single-implementation-plan-shaped (no decomposition needed); ambiguity check fixed three call-outs.

Spec awaiting Hemanth review. Writing-plans + executing-plans phases fire separately. No src/ touched; no implementation code; no commit; no smoke. Carry-forward: 32 prior RTCs unswept since 0584885 (this is #33; brainstorm-only RTC adds doc-only).

READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 5, FOCUS_RECT_KILL_APP_WIDE 2026-05-12 ~09:35pm — Hemanth flagged cyan-blue vertical bars at column boundaries on selected rows of StreamDetailView's episode table, visible "everywhere in the app every time I select a file." Diagnosis (no /superpowers:systematic-debugging multi-pass needed — bug class is well-known Qt/Win11 native-style leak): the bars are Windows 11's native `QStyle::PE_FrameFocusRect` per-cell focus rectangle, painted by the native style primitive rather than the QSS path. Our existing Theme.cpp `outline: 0` rule on `QTableWidget, QListWidget, QTreeWidget` (line 521) only suppresses the QSS-driven outline; native PE_FrameFocusRect ignores it. The rect picks up the OS system accent (cyan-blue on Win11) and renders as a thin 1-2px border on each focused cell — visible as vertical bars at column boundaries on a row with cell-by-cell focus iteration. Confirmed NOT one of: (a) selection-background-color (gold __ACCENT_SOFT__ in Dark mode, plus StreamDetailView explicitly overrides to rgba(255,255,255,0.08) at StreamDetailView.cpp:580 — neither is cyan); (b) gridlines (setShowGrid(false) is set on the episode table); (c) hardcoded cyan literal in any src/ file (only #88c0d0 exists, that's the Nord-mode frost accent token in a different palette path). Fix uses a single global chokepoint that catches every QTableView/QTableWidget/QListView/QListWidget/QTreeView/QTreeWidget/QComboBox-popup in one stroke without per-widget hunting across the 54 files that instantiate item-view widgets: (1) NEW `NoFocusFrameStyle : public QProxyStyle` class in Theme.cpp anonymous namespace — overrides `drawPrimitive` and early-returns when element == PE_FrameFocusRect, forwards every other primitive through to the wrapped style (zero behavior change for buttons/scrollbars/checkboxes/splitters/frames/etc.). Installed once via `app.setStyle(new NoFocusFrameStyle)` at the top of `applyTheme()`, guarded by a static bool so theme switches don't stack proxies. setStyle takes ownership; QProxyStyle's default ctor wraps the application's current style (the platform default on Win11). (2) Defense-in-depth QSS additions to Theme.cpp template after the existing item-view block: `QAbstractItemView { outline: 0; }` + `QAbstractItemView::item:focus { outline: 0; border: 0; }` + explicit `QTableWidget::item:focus, QListWidget::item:focus, QTreeWidget::item:focus { outline: 0; border: 0; }` — these catch any style path that bypasses the proxy. (3) Belt-and-braces in the three existing custom `QStyledItemDelegate::paint` overrides — strip `QStyle::State_HasFocus` from a local copy of `option.state` before forwarding to base paint: ProgressIconDelegate (SeriesView.cpp:33), ShowProgressIconDelegate (ShowView.cpp:39), ChapterStatusIconDelegate (MangaTransferDialog.cpp:35). Ensures any future delegate-painted focus indicator over the icon stays suppressed too. ZERO per-widget changes — every item-view surface app-wide (StreamDetailView episodes, TankorentPage transfers, TorrentFilesTab files, TankoyomiPage chapters, MangaTransferDialog, ShowView/SeriesView episode tables, KeybindingEditor, PlaylistDrawer, SubtitlePopover/AudioPopover lists, AddTorrentDialog, HistoryDialog, IndexerStatusPanel, CategoryEditorPage, BookResultsGrid/TransfersView, CalendarScreen, AddonManagerScreen, LibraryListView, every QComboBox popup) is covered by the single proxy-style install. Mode coverage: Dark + Light + Nord + Solarized + Gruvbox + Catppuccin all benefit equally — fix is at the style layer below palette/QSS. Total surface: 4 files, ~45 LOC. Theme.cpp BUILD OK 13s incremental (build_check.bat first try). No native_sidecar touched. /superpowers:verification-before-completion: pre-fix screenshot shows cyan bars at every cell boundary on selected row in StreamDetailView; post-fix expectation is clean selection background (gold-alpha in Dark mode, white-alpha on StreamDetailView's per-widget override) with NO vertical bars at column edges across every item view in the app. Hemanth smoke gate covers ~17 surfaces enumerated above; if any specific delegate's icon paint still leaks a focus rect, that's a missed custom-paint path and I'll patch it. Theme persistence + theme-switch + topbar picker behavior preserved (proxy install is idempotent via s_proxyStyleInstalled static bool). Recovery: revert is trivial — remove the s_proxyStyleInstalled gate + setStyle call + class definition; everything else is additive.] | Skills invoked: [/build-verify, /superpowers:verification-before-completion, /superpowers:requesting-code-review, /simplify] | files: src/ui/Theme.cpp, src/ui/pages/SeriesView.cpp, src/ui/pages/ShowView.cpp, src/ui/dialogs/MangaTransferDialog.cpp, agents/chat.md

READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 4, CW_NAMESPACE_BOUNDARY — stream→video Continue Watching leak fix 2026-05-13 ~5:15pm — Hemanth report: "The shows that I watch on stream mode appear in the Continue Watching of Video mode. That should not happen. Video mode is for videos in the video library and Stream mode is for streaming videos AND the downloaded streams." /superpowers:systematic-debugging Phase 1-4: (Phase 1 evidence) Dumped per-domain progress stores on disk. video_progress.json 28.9KB keyed by SHA-1 hashes with `path` field; stream_progress.json 585B keyed by `stream:<imdb>` / `stream:<imdb>:s<N>:e<N>` schema. Confirmed the stores are SEPARATE — leak must be a double-write at a launch site, not a unified store. (Phase 2 root cause) Traced two playback launch paths: pure-stream HTTP via StreamPage::onReadyToPlay correctly sets PersistenceMode::None on VideoPlayer + subscribes its own progressUpdated lambda that writes "stream" domain via StreamPage.cpp:2972; downloaded-stream auto-play (Layer 3 Rule C) via MainWindow::onPlayLocalFileFromStreamRequested Q_UNUSED'd the imdbId/season/episode args and called openVideoPlayer(localPath) — same path Video-mode library uses — leaving the player in default PersistenceMode::LibraryVideos. Because the file exists on disk, videoIdForFile() resolves, m_currentVideoId is non-empty, and the gate at VideoPlayer.cpp:3193 (`if (m_persistenceMode == LibraryVideos && !m_currentVideoId.isEmpty())`) PASSES → saveProgress("videos", videoId, data) fires per-tick → entry created in video_progress.json with a `path` matching the downloaded-stream canonical file → VideosPage::collectContinueItems (VideosPage.cpp:1410) reads allProgress("videos"), filters by `finished/positionSec/QFile::exists(filePath)` — all true for a recently-watched downloaded-stream file — and renders the entry on Video-mode CW. The pre-existing TODO comment at MainWindow.cpp:962-967 had flagged this exact follow-up: "imdbId/season/episode args are kept on the signal so a future iteration can teach VideoPlayer to write Stream-domain progress under the canonical stream:imdb:sNN:eMM key (Spec §10.1 Continue Watching surfacing) — that bridging is a separable follow-up". (Phase 3-4 fix) Pick: write-site redirection at the launch site + one-shot startup migration. Smallest blast radius, no coupling between VideoPlayer and StreamDownloadIndex, no Agent 5 surface (VideosPage.cpp) touched. Three edits in MainWindow.{h,cpp} + one include: (1) `#include "core/stream/StreamProgress.h"`. (2) Header gains private member `QMetaObject::Connection m_streamPlaybackProgressConn` + private method `void migrateLegacyStreamProgressEntries()` with rationale comment. (3) openVideoPlayer() body now disconnects any prior stream-progress conn + resets PersistenceMode to LibraryVideos at top — guarantees plain library plays land in "videos" domain even after a downloaded-stream session. (4) onPlayLocalFileFromStreamRequested rewired: removed `Q_UNUSED(imdbId/season/episode)`; call openVideoPlayer(localPath) FIRST (which sets clean library-videos defaults via the new top block), then setPersistenceMode(None) (suppresses the videos write at VideoPlayer.cpp:3193), then connect VideoPlayer::progressUpdated → lambda that builds epKey via StreamProgress::episodeKey (series) or movieKey (movie), gates on 5s posSec floor (matches StreamPage.cpp:2963), computes finished from durSec/posSec >= 0.9 threshold, calls makeWatchState + bridge->saveProgress("stream", epKey, state). Connection stored in m_streamPlaybackProgressConn for tear-down on next openVideoPlayer call. (5) migrateLegacyStreamProgressEntries() — one-shot sweep called from MainWindow ctor right after m_streamDownloadIndex construction, before buildPageStack. Walks allProgress("videos"); for each entry computes canonicalKey from `path` field; if it matches a StreamDownloadIndex::Entry (built once into a local QHash<QString, Entry> from index->all() for O(1) lookup), computes the equivalent stream epKey, compares updatedAt against any pre-existing "stream"-domain entry — if videos entry is newer, seeds "stream" with makeWatchState; either way clearProgress("videos", videoId). Logged DebugLogBuffer summary {migratedToStream, evictedNoSeed}. Net effect: legacy polluted entries get cleaned + their progress state preserved in the right namespace so Stream-mode CW picks up where the user left off. build_check.bat BUILD OK first-try. NO Agent 5 surface touched (VideosPage.cpp's collectContinueItems unchanged); the boundary is enforced upstream (write-site + migration). Hemanth smoke gate covers verification matrix (a) stream HTTP played show → not in Video CW / in Stream CW ✓ unchanged from existing correct behavior; (b) downloaded-stream played episode → not in Video CW (migration evicts legacy, lambda redirects new) / in Stream CW (lambda seeds "stream:imdb:sN:eM"); (c) plain library file → in Video CW only ✓ unchanged (LibraryVideos default); (d) existing polluted entry → migrated on next launch, no manual cleanup needed. Anti-patterns honored: did not widen to other cross-mode leaks; did not reintroduce per-category CW; did not edit VideosPage.cpp silently. Resume position for downloaded-stream playback is a separate concern (VideoPlayer's openFile resume-seek currently reads from "videos" domain by videoId — after this fix that read returns empty for downloaded streams; resume from "stream" domain by epKey would require a VideoPlayer→StreamDownloadIndex coupling that we explicitly avoided; flag for follow-up if Hemanth surfaces it). References: feedback_evidence_before_analysis.md (read evidence first — disk dump confirmed schema split), feedback_session_lifecycle_pattern.md (this WAS a session-lifecycle race in the sense that two launch paths produced different player state), STREAM_DOWNLOADED_LIBRARY P4 follow-up TODO comment at MainWindow.cpp:962 (which predicted this exact fix), feedback_agent5_scope.md (avoided editing Agent 5's VideosPage.cpp surface).] | Skills invoked: [/superpowers:systematic-debugging, /simplify, /build-verify, /superpowers:verification-before-completion, /superpowers:requesting-code-review] | files: src/ui/MainWindow.h, src/ui/MainWindow.cpp, agents/chat.md

READY TO COMMIT - [Agent 4, SUB_URL_DENYLIST_FIX — addon subtitle URLs unblocked 2026-05-13 ~6:20pm — Hemanth report: "unsupported subtitle extension in url:http... that is an error I get every time I try to load the other subs from the addon, can you please fix this issue?". /superpowers:systematic-debugging Phase 1-4: (Phase 1 evidence) grep located the exact error string at SidecarProcess.cpp:881-883 inside sendSetSubtitleUrl. (Phase 2-3 root cause) The precheck at line 881 called isSubtitleExtension(url.path()) — an allowlist function at line 770 that accepted only srt/vtt/ass/ssa/sub extensions. Modern Stremio subtitle addons (OpenSubtitles v3 REST API, Subscene-style URLs, addons that hide format in query params, addons that 302-redirect through CDNs) commonly serve subtitles from URLs that have NO extension on the path component (e.g., `/api/v3/subtitles/abc123`), an UNKNOWN extension (`.cgi`, `.aspx`, `.bin`), or where the path's `QFileInfo::suffix()` returns the redirect target's intermediate token. All such URLs got rejected with "Unsupported subtitle extension in URL: …" before the GET ever fired. The download-completion handler at line 919-923 already handles extension-less URLs correctly — it defaults the staging temp file's extension to `srt` when `QFileInfo(url.path()).suffix()` is empty. So the precheck was the only thing blocking; the rest of the pipeline (libass content-sniffing on the sidecar side) tolerates the lack of a definitive extension. (Phase 4 fix) Inverted allowlist → denylist. Function renamed isSubtitleExtension → hasObviouslyNonSubtitleExtension. Now returns true ONLY when the URL's path has a clearly-not-a-subtitle extension drawn from a static QSet covering: video (mkv/mp4/avi/mov/wmv/flv/webm/m4v/mpg/mpeg/ts/m2ts/mts/vob/ogv/3gp), audio (mp3/aac/flac/wav/ogg/opus/m4a/wma), image (jpg/jpeg/png/gif/webp/bmp/tiff/svg), archive (zip/rar/7z/tar/gz/bz2), document/page (pdf/html/htm/xml/css/js), executable/package (exe/dll/app/apk/deb/rpm). Empty extension → returns false (passes through). Unknown extension → returns false (passes through). Call site at line 881 inverted from `if (!isSubtitleExtension(...))` to `if (hasObviouslyNonSubtitleExtension(...))`. Error message updated to "URL appears to point to a non-subtitle file (extension .X): URL" — includes the rejected extension so future debugging surfaces WHICH extension tripped the denylist instead of the prior vague "Unsupported subtitle extension". Side effects: any addon URL that previously got rejected by the precheck now reaches the download path; download succeeds → temp file staged (with .srt default if URL had no extension) → sendLoadExternalSub fires → sidecar's libass parser handles SRT/VTT/ASS/SSA via content sniffing on the bytes regardless of staging filename. If the URL returns garbage (HTML error page, empty body), the existing downstream guards at line 904-916 catch it ("Subtitle download failed" / "Subtitle download empty payload"). build_check.bat BUILD OK first-try. No header changes (function is in anonymous namespace inside the .cpp; only call site is internal to the same .cpp). No sidecar protocol change. No engine API change. References: feedback_evidence_before_analysis.md (grepped error string before forming hypothesis), feedback_one_fix_per_rebuild.md (single surgical change, didn't widen to add Content-Type sniffing in the download handler — that's a separable hardening pass if HTML-error-page payloads start showing up as silent parse failures). Hemanth smoke gate: open the player on a stream with subtitles, click any addon-sourced subtitle from the Tracks popover. Pre-fix: "Unsupported subtitle extension in URL: ..." error toast. Post-fix: subtitle should load + render. If a specific addon URL still fails, the new error message will surface the extension so we can decide whether to add it to the denylist (unlikely — the existing denylist is comprehensive) or whether it's a downstream parse failure (handled by the existing post-download guards).] | Skills invoked: [/superpowers:systematic-debugging, /simplify, /build-verify, /superpowers:verification-before-completion, /superpowers:requesting-code-review] | files: src/ui/player/SidecarProcess.cpp, agents/chat.md
