After Remove on a Published row, cancelStreamBulkItem(groupId, itemKey, deleteFile=true) sets item state to kStateCancelled, calls deleteTorrent (removes libtorrent record + file from disk), and evicts StreamDownloadIndex. Item's infoHash field is NOT cleared. (Phase 2 root cause) resolveRowState maps Cancelled (which is in isTerminalCohortState's set) → RowState::Failed → action icon shows retry-arrow + "Retry" tooltip. User clicks Retry → onActionIconClicked's Failed branch fires retryStreamBulkGroupFailedItems(groupId, itemKey). Inside that function (TorrentClient.cpp:1236), the per-item loop gates on isStreamBulkSourceRetryState (line 93-99) which accepts MissingSource / MetadataFailed / Failed-with-empty-infoHash / Pending-with-empty-infoHash but DOES NOT include Cancelled (by design — Cancelled is a user-driven terminal state, not a recovery candidate). For the Cancelled item: state="Cancelled", infoHash=old-stale-hash → isStreamBulkSourceRetryState returns false; the publishFailed branch doesn't match; the Failed-with-torrent-error branch doesn't match. Loop iteration is a no-op for this item. Function returns having only bumped retryGeneration. Hence: visible Retry button does nothing. (Phase 3 hypothesis + 4 fix) Hemanth's mental model for Remove→Retry is "give me a fresh download of this episode" — equivalent semantically to clicking an Idle (never-downloaded) row. NOT "rerun source-pick on the stale cohort entry" which is what retryStreamBulkGroupFailedItems does. Implementation pick: discriminate Cancelled inside onActionIconClicked's Failed branch (single edit in StreamDetailView.cpp); route Cancelled → emit singleEpisodeDownloadRequested (same path as Idle click → StreamPage::onSingleEpisodeDownloadRequested → triggerBulkSelectedEpisodes → orchestrator fan-out + fresh dispatch). Other terminal failures (MissingSource / MetadataFailed / Failed / Orphaned / PublishFailed) keep the existing retry-path semantics — for those the source-pick-rerun IS the right operation. Re-reads the cohort state via streamBulkSnapshotForImdbSeason at click time so the discrimination always reflects current data (the cohort row state could have transitioned between row-paint and click; the snapshot is the truth). build_check.bat BUILD OK first-try. NO engine change (TorrentClient untouched), NO header change, single-method edit. Considered + rejected: (a) adding kStateCancelled to isStreamBulkSourceRetryState — would muddy the predicate's meaning (Cancelled isn't a "source retry" state; it's a "fresh dispatch" state); plus it'd require also clearing the cohort item's infoHash field in cancelStreamBulkItem to satisfy the empty-infoHash sub-gate. (b) extending the RowState enum with a Cancelled variant — bigger blast radius, requires touching resolveRowState + actionIconForState + the icon mapping; user-visible behavior is identical to (Failed→retry icon→fresh dispatch). (c) editing cancelStreamBulkItem to clear infoHash on Cancelled items — orthogonal concern; the cohort still won't dispatch a retry under the existing predicate without ALSO adding Cancelled to it. Single-edit fix at the click site is the minimum-blast-radius path. Edge cases verified by reasoning: (1) single-item season with the only item Cancelled then GC'd by the post-cancel cleanup loop → cohort entry gone → resolveRowState falls through cohortHit=false → returns Idle → Idle click already does singleEpisodeDownloadRequested. The fix is unnecessary for this case (already worked). (2) Multi-item season with one item Cancelled, rest active → cohort survives → that item appears as Failed → fix routes to fresh dispatch ✓. (3) Other terminal states (MissingSource etc.) → fix's gate (cohortState == "Cancelled") is false → falls through to existing retry path ✓. References: feedback_evidence_before_analysis.md (traced predicate code before forming the fix), feedback_one_fix_per_rebuild.md (single surgical edit), STREAM_DOWNLOADS_NETFLIX_OVERHAUL P3 revision RTC 2026-05-13 12:50pm (the Cancel→Remove label morph that produced this Cancelled-state UX). Hemanth smoke gate: open a downloaded episode in StreamDetailView, click the tick → Remove → confirm; row should re-display with retry-arrow action icon; click retry → fresh source-pick + dispatch (toast / cohort-state update visible). Pre-fix: no visible effect on click. Post-fix: download restarts.] | Skills invoked: [/superpowers:systematic-debugging, /simplify, /build-verify, /superpowers:verification-before-completion, /superpowers:requesting-code-review] | files: src/ui/pages/stream/StreamDetailView.cpp, agents/chat.md
